sāmaveda saṃhitā
kauthuma śākhā

pūrvārcikaḥ
chanda ārcikaḥ

āgneyaṃ kāṇḍam
prathamaḥ prapāṭhakaḥ  |  prathamo'rdhaḥ
1 1 1 0101   agna ā yāhi vītaye gṛṇāno havyadātaye  |
1 1 1 0101  ni hotā satsi barhiṣi  | | 1
1 1 1 0101   tvamagne yajñānāṃ hotā viśveṣāṃ hitaḥ  |
1 1 1 0102  devebhirmānuṣe jane  | | 2
1 1 1 0103   agniṃ dūtaṃ vṛṇīmahe hotāraṃ viśvavedasam  |
1 1 1 0103  asya yajñasya sukratum  | | 3
1 1 1 0104   agnirvṛtrāṇi jaṅghanaddraviṇasyurvipanyayā  |
1 1 1 0104  samiddhaḥ śukra āhutaḥ  | | 4
1 1 1 0105   preṣṭhaṃ vo atithiṃ stuṣe mitramiva priyam  |
1 1 1 0105  agne rathaṃ na vedyam  | | 5
1 1 1 0106   tvaṃ no agne mahobhiḥ pāhi viśvasyā arāteḥ  |
1 1 1 0106  uta dviṣo martyasya  | | 6
1 1 1 0107   ehyū ṣu bravāṇi te'gna itthetarā giraḥ  |
1 1 1 0107  ebhirvardhāsa indubhiḥ  | | 7
1 1 1 0108   ā te vatso mano yamatparamāccitsadhasthāt |
1 1 1 0108  agne tvāṃ kāmaye girā  | | 8
1 1 1 0109   tvāmagne puṣkarādadhyatharvā niramanthata  |
1 1 1 0109  mūrdhno viśvasya vāghataḥ  | | 9
1 1 1 0110   agne vivasvadā bharāsmabhyamūtaye mahe  |
1 1 1 0110  devo hyasi no dṛśe  | | 10

1 1 1 0201   namaste agna ojase gṛṇanti deva kṛṣṭayaḥ  |
1 1 1 0201  amairamitramardaya  | | 11
1 1 1 0202   dūtaṃ vo viśvavedasaṃ havyavāhamamartyam  |
1 1 1 0202  yajiṣṭhamṛñjase girā  | | 12
1 1 1 0203   upa tvā jāmayo giro dediśatīrhaviṣkṛtaḥ  |
1 1 1 0203  vāyoranīke asthiran | | 13
1 1 1 0204   upa tvāgne divedive doṣāvastardhiyā vayam  |
1 1 1 0204  namo bharanta emasi  | | 14
1 1 1 0205   jarābodha tadviviḍḍhi viśeviśe yajñiyāya  |
1 1 1 0205  stomaṃ rudrāya dṛśīkam  | | 15
1 1 1 0206   prati tyaṃ cārumadhvaraṃ gopīthāya pra hūyase  |
1 1 1 0206  marudbhiragna ā gahi  | | 16
1 1 1 0207   aśvaṃ na tvā vāravantaṃ vandadhyā agniṃ namobhiḥ  |
1 1 1 0207  samrājantamadhvarāṇām  | | 17
1 1 1 0208   aurvabhṛguvacchucimapnavānavadā huve  |
1 1 1 0208  agniṃ samudravāsasam  | | 18
1 1 1 0209   agnimindhāno manasā dhiyaṃ saceta martyaḥ  |
1 1 1 0209  agnimindhe vivasvabhiḥ  | | 19
1 1 1 0210   āditpratnasya retaso jyotiḥ paśyanti vāsaram  |
1 1 1 0210  paro yadidhyate divi  | | 20

1 1 1 0301   agniṃ vo vṛdhantamadhvarāṇāṃ purūtamam  |
1 1 1 0301  acchā naptre sahasvate  | | 21
1 1 1 0302   agnistigmena śociṣā yaṃsadviśvaṃ nyā3 riṇam  |
1 1 1 0302  agnirno vaṃsate rayim  | | 22
1 1 1 0303   agne mṛḍa mahāṃ asyaya ā devayuṃ janam  |
1 1 1 0303  iyetha barhirāsadam  | | 23
1 1 1 0304   agne rakṣā ṇo aṃhasaḥ prati sma deva rīṣataḥ  |
1 1 1 0304  tapiṣṭhairajaro daha  | | 24
1 1 1 0305   agne yuṅkṣvā hi ye tavāśvāso deva sādhavaḥ  |
1 1 1 0305  araṃ vahantyāśavaḥ  | | 25
1 1 1 0306   ni tvā nakṣya viśpate dyumantaṃ dhīmahe vayam  |
1 1 1 0306  suvīramagna āhuta  | | 26
1 1 1 0307   agnirmūrdhā divaḥ kakutpatiḥ pṛthivyā ayam  |
1 1 1 0307  apāṃ retāṃsi jinvati  | | 27
1 1 1 0308   imamū ṣu tvamasmākaṃ saniṃ gāyatraṃ navyāṃsam  |
1 1 1 0308  agne deveṣu pra vocaḥ  | | 28
1 1 1 0309   taṃ tvā gopavano girā janiṣṭhadagne aṅgaraḥ  |
1 1 1 0309  sa pāvaka śrudhī havam  | | 29
1 1 1 0310   pari vājapatiḥ kaviragnirhavyānyakramīt |
1 1 1 0310  dadhadratnāni dāśuṣe  | | 30
1 1 1 0311   udu tyaṃ jātavedasaṃ devaṃ vahanti ketavaḥ  |
1 1 1 0311  dṛśe viśvāya sūryam  | | 31
1 1 1 0312   kavimagnimupa stuhi satyadharmāṇamadhvare  |
1 1 1 0312  devamamīvacātanam  | | 32
1 1 1 0313   śaṃ no devīrabhiṣṭaye śaṃ no bhavantu pītaye  |
1 1 1 0313  śaṃ yorabhi sravantu naḥ  | | 33
1 1 1 0314   kasya nūnaṃ parīṇasi dhiyo jinvasi satpate  |
1 1 1 0314  joṣātā yasya te giraḥ  | | 34

1 1 1 0401   yajñāyajñā vo agnaye girāgirā ca dakṣase  |
1 1 1 0401  prapra vayamamṛtaṃ jātavedasaṃ priyaṃ mitraṃ na śaṃsiṣam  | | 35
1 1 1 0402   pāhi no agna ekayā pāhyū3 a dvitīyayā  |
1 1 1 0402  pāhi gīrbhistisṛbhirūrjāṃ pate pāhi catasṛbhirvaso  | | 36
1 1 1 0403   bṛhadbhiragne arcibhiḥ śukreṇa deva śociṣā  |
1 1 1 0403  bharadvāje samidhāno yaviṣṭhya revatpāvaka dīdihi  | | 37
1 1 1 0404   tve agne svāhuta priyāsaḥ santu sūrayaḥ  |
1 1 1 0404  yantāro ye maghavāno janānāmūrvaṃ dayanta gonām  | | 38
1 1 1 0405   agne jaritarviśpatistapāno deva rakṣasaḥ  |
1 1 1 0405  aproṣivāngṛhapate mahāṃ asi divaspāyurduroṇayuḥ  | | 39
1 1 1 0406   agne vivasvaduṣasaścitraṃ rādho amartya  |
1 1 1 0406  ā dāśuṣe jātavedo vahā tvamadyā devāṃ uṣarbudhaḥ  | | 40
1 1 1 0407   tvaṃ naścitra ūtyā vaso rādhāṃsi codaya  |
1 1 1 0407  asya rāyastvamagne rathīrasi vidā gādhaṃ tuce tu naḥ  | | 41
1 1 1 0408   tvamitsaprathā asyagne trātarṛtaḥ kaviḥ  |
1 1 1 0408  tvāṃ viprāsaḥ samidhāna dīdiva ā vivāsanti vedhasaḥ  | | 42
1 1 1 0409   ā no agne vayovṛdhaṃ rayiṃ pāvaka śaṃsyam  |
1 1 1 0409  rāsvā ca na upamāte puruspṛhaṃ sunītī suyaśastaram  | | 43
1 1 1 0410   yo viśvā dayate vasu hotā mandro janānām  |
1 1 1 0410  madhorna pātrā prathamānyasmai pra stomā yantvagnaye  | | 44

1 1 1 0501   enā vo agniṃ namasorjo napātamā huve  |
1 1 1 0501  priyaṃ cetiṣṭhamaratiṃ svādhvaraṃ viśvasya dūtamamṛtam  | | 45
1 1 1 0502   śeṣe vaneṣu mātṛṣu saṃ tvā martāsa indhate  |
1 1 1 0502  atandro havyaṃ vahasi haviṣkṛta ādiddeveṣu rājasi  | | 46
1 1 1 0503   adarśi gātuvittamo yasminvratānyādadhuḥ  |
1 1 1 0503  upo ṣu jātamāryasya vardhanamagniṃ nakṣantu no giraḥ  | | 47
1 1 1 0504   agnirukthe purohito grāvāṇo barhiradhvare  |
1 1 1 0504  ṛcā yāmi maruto brahmaṇaspate devā avo vareṇyam  | | 48
1 1 1 0505   agnimīḍiṣvāvase gāthābhiḥ śīraśociṣam  |
1 1 1 0505  agniṃ rāye purumīḍha śrutaṃ naro'gniḥ sudītaye chardiḥ  | | 49
1 1 1 0506   śrudhi śrutkarṇa vahnibhirdevairagne sayāvabhiḥ  |
1 1 1 0506  ā sīdatu barhiṣi mitro aryamā prātaryāvabhiradhvare  | | 50
1 1 1 0507   pra daivodāso agnirdeva indro na majmanā  |
1 1 1 0507  anu mātaraṃ pṛthivīṃ vi vāvṛte tasthau nākasya śarmaṇi  | | 51
1 1 1 0508   adha jmo adha vā divo bṛhato rocanādadhi  |
1 1 1 0508  ayā vardhasva tanvā girā mamā jātā sukrato pṛṇa  | | 52
1 1 1 0509   kāyamāno vanā tvaṃ yanmātṛīrajagannapaḥ  |
1 1 1 0509  na tatte agne pramṛṣe nivartanaṃ yaddūre sannihābhuvaḥ  | | 53
1 1 1 0510   ni tvāmagne manurdadhe jyotirjanāya śaśvate  |
1 1 1 0510  dīdetha kaṇva ṛtajāta ukṣito yaṃ namasyanti kṛṣṭayaḥ  | | 54

prathama prapāṭhakaḥ  |  dvitīyo'rdhaḥ
1 1 2 0601   devo vo draviṇodāḥ pūrṇāṃ vivaṣṭvāsicam  |
1 1 2 0601  udvā siñjadhvamupa vā pṛṇadhvamādidvo deva ohate  | | 55
1 1 2 0602   praitu brahmaṇaspatiḥ pra devyetu sūnṛtā  |
1 1 2 0602  acchā vīraṃ naryaṃ paṅktirādhasaṃ devā yajñaṃ nayantu naḥ  | | 56
1 1 2 0603   ūrdhva ū ṣu ṇa ūtaye tiṣṭhā devo na savitā  |
1 1 2 0603  ūrdhvo vājasya sanitā yadañjibhirvāghadbhirvihvayāmahe  | | 57
1 1 2 0604   pra yo rāye ninīṣati marto yaste vaso dāśat |
1 1 2 0604  sa vīraṃ dhatte agna ukthaśaṃsinaṃ tmanā sahasrapoṣiṇam  | | 58
1 1 2 0605   pra vo yahvaṃ purūṇāṃ viśāṃ devayatīnām  |
1 1 2 0605  agniṃ sūktebhirvacobhirvṛṇīmahe yaṃsamidanya indhate  | | 59
1 1 2 0606   ayamagniḥ suvīryasyeśe hi saubhagasya  |
1 1 2 0606  rāya īśe svapatyasya gomata īśe vṛtrahathānām  | | 60
1 1 2 0607   tvamagne gṛhapatistvaṃ hotā no adhvare  |
1 1 2 0607  tvaṃ potā viśvavāra pracetā tākṣi yāsi ca vāryam  | | 61
1 1 2 0608   sakhāyastvā vavṛmahe devaṃ martāsa ūtaye  |
1 1 2 0608  apāṃ napātaṃ subhagaṃ sudaṃsasaṃ supratūrtimanehasam  | | 62

1 1 2 0701   ā juhotā haviṣā marjayadhvaṃ ni hotāraṃ gṛhapatiṃ dadhidhvam  |
1 1 2 0701  iḍaspade namasā rātahavyaṃ saparyatā yajataṃ pastyānām  | | 63
1 1 2 0702   citra icchiśostaruṇasya vakṣatho na yo mātarāvanveti dhātave  |
1 1 2 0702  anūdhā yadajījanadadhā cidā vavakṣatsadyo mahi dūtyā3ṃ caran | | 64
1 1 2 0703   idaṃ ta ekaṃ para ū ta ekaṃ tṛtīyena jyotiṣā saṃ viśasva  |
1 1 2 0703  saṃveśanastanve3 cāruredhi priyo devānāṃ parame janitre  | | 65
1 1 2 0704   imaṃ stomamarhate jātavedase rathamiva saṃ mahemā manīṣayā  |
1 1 2 0704  bhadrā hi naḥ pramatirasya saṃsadyagne sakhye mā riṣāmā vayaṃ tava  | | 66
1 1 2 0705   mūrdhānaṃ divo aratiṃ pṛthivyā vaiśvānaramṛta ā jātamagnim  |
1 1 2 0705  kaviṃ samrājamatithiṃ janānāmāsannāḥ pātraṃ janayanta devāḥ  | | 67
1 1 2 0706   vi tvadāpo na parvatasya pṛṣṭhādukthebhiragne janayanta devāḥ  |
1 1 2 0706  taṃ tvā giraḥ suṣṭutayo vājayantyājiṃ na girvavāho jigyuraśvāḥ  | | 68
1 1 2 0707   ā vo rājānamadhvarasya rudraṃ hotāraṃ satyayajaṃ rodasyoḥ  |
1 1 2 0707  agniṃ purā tanayitnoracittāddhiraṇyarūpamavase kṛṇudhvam  | | 69
1 1 2 0708   indhe rājā samaryo namobhiryasya pratīkamāhutaṃ ghṛtena  |
1 1 2 0708  naro havyebhirīḍate sabādha āgniragramuṣasāmaśoci  | | 70
1 1 2 0709   pra ketunā bṛhatā yātyagnirā rodasī vṛṣabho roravīti  |
1 1 2 0709  divaścidantādupamāmudānaḍapāmupasthe mahiṣo vavardha  | | 71
1 1 2 0710   agniṃ naro dīdhitibhiraṇyorhastacyutaṃ janayata praśastam  |
1 1 2 0710  dūredṛśaṃ gṛhapatimathavyum  | | 72

1 1 2 0801   abodhyagniḥ samidhā janānāṃ prati dhenumivāyatīmuṣāsam  |
1 1 2 0801  yahvā iva pra vayāmujjihānāḥ pra bhānavaḥ sasrate nākamaccha  | | 73
1 1 2 0802   pra bhūrjayantaṃ mahāṃ vipodhāṃ mūrairamūraṃ purāṃ darmāṇam  |
1 1 2 0802  nayantaṃ gīrbhirvanā dhiyaṃ dhā hariśmaśruṃ na vārmaṇā dhanarcim  | | 74
1 1 2 0803   śukraṃ te anyadyajataṃ te anyadviṣurūpe ahanī dyaurivāsi  |
1 1 2 0803  viśvā hi māyā avasi svadhāvanbhadrā te pūṣanniha rātirastu | | 75
1 1 2 0804   iḍāmagne purudaṃsaṃ saniṃ goḥ śaśvattamaṃ havamānāya sādha  |
1 1 2 0804  syānnaḥ sūnustanayo vijāvāgne sā te sumatirbhūtvasme  | | 76
1 1 2 0805   pra hotā jāto mahānnabhovinnṛṣadmā sīdadapāṃ vivarte  |
1 1 2 0805  dadhadyo dhāyī sute vayāṃsi yantā vasūni vidhate tanūpāḥ  | | 77
1 1 2 0806   pra samrājamasurasya praśstaṃ puṃsaḥ kṛṣṭīnāmanumādyasya  |
1 1 2 0806  indrasyeva pra tavasaskṛtāni vandadvārā vandamānā vivaṣṭu  | | 78
1 1 2 0807   araṇyornihito jātavedā garbha ivetsubhṛto garbhiṇībhiḥ  |
1 1 2 0807  divediva īḍyo jāgṛvadbhirhaviṣmadbhirmanuṣyebhiragniḥ  | | 79
1 1 2 0808   sanādagne mṛṇasi yātudhānānna tvā rakṣāṃsi pṛtanāsu jigyuḥ  |
1 1 2 0808  anu daha sahamūrānkayādo mā te hetyā mukṣata daivyāyāḥ  | | 80

1 1 2 0901   agna ojiṣṭhamā bhara dyumnamasmabhyamadhrigo  |
1 1 2 0901  pra no rāye panīyase ratsi vājāya panthām  | | 81
1 1 2 0902   yadi vīro anu ṣyādagnimindhīta martyaḥ  |
1 1 2 0902  ājuhvaddhavyamānuṣakśarma bhakṣīta daivyam  | | 82
1 1 2 0903   tveṣaste dhūma ṛṇvati divi saṃ cchukra ātataḥ  |
1 1 2 0903  sūro na hi dyutā tvaṃ kṛpā pāvaka rocase  | | 83
1 1 2 0904   tvaṃ hi kṣaitavadyaśo'gne mitro na patyase  |
1 1 2 0904  tvaṃ vicarṣaṇe śravo vaso puṣṭiṃ na puṣyasi  | | 84
1 1 2 0905   prātaragniḥ purupriyo viṣa stavetātithiḥ  |
1 1 2 0905  viśve yasminnamartye havyaṃ martāsa indhate  | | 85
1 1 2 0906   yadvāhiṣṭhaṃ tadagnaye bṛhadarca vibhāvaso  |
1 1 2 0906  mahiṣīva tvadrayistvadvājā udīrate  | | 86
1 1 2 0907   viśoviśo vo atithiṃ vājayantaḥ purupriyam  |
1 1 2 0907  agniṃ vo duryaṃ vacaḥ stuṣe śūṣasya manmabhiḥ  | | 87
1 1 2 0908   bṛhadvayo hi bhānave'rcā devāyāgnaye  |
1 1 2 0908  yaṃ mitraṃ na praśastaye martāso dadhire puraḥ  | | 88
1 1 2 0909   aganma vṛtrahantamaṃ jyeṣṭhamagnimānavam  |
1 1 2 0909  ya sma śrutarvannārkṣe bṛhadanīka idhyate  | | 89
1 1 2 0910   jātaḥ pareṇa dharmaṇā yatsavṛdbhiḥ sahābhuvaḥ  |
1 1 2 0910  pitā yatkaśyapasyāgniḥ śraddhā mātā manuḥ kaviḥ  | | 90

1 1 2 1001   somaṃ rājānaṃ varuṇamagnimanvārabhāmahe  |
1 1 2 1001  ādityaṃ viṣṇuṃ sūryaṃ brahmānaṃ ca bṛhaspatim  | | 91
1 1 2 1002   ita eta udāruhandivaḥ pṛṣṭhānyā ruhan |
1 1 2 1002  pra bhūrjayo yathā pathodyāmaṅgiraso yayuḥ  | | 92
1 1 2 1003   rāye agne mahe tvā dānāya samidhīmahi  |
1 1 2 1003  īḍiṣvā hi mahe vṛṣaṃ dyāvā hotrāya pṛthivī  | | 93
1 1 2 1004   dadhanve vā yadīmanu vocadbrahmeti veru tat |
1 1 2 1004  pari viśvāni kāvyā nemiścakramivābhuvat | | 94
1 1 2 1005   pratyagne harasā haraḥ śṛṇāhi viśvataspari  |
1 1 2 1005  yātudhānasya rakṣaso balaṃ nyubjavīryam  | | 95
1 1 2 1006   tvamagne vasūṃriha rudrāṃ ādityāṃ uta  |
1 1 2 1006  yajā svadhvaraṃ janaṃ manujātaṃ ghṛtapruṣam  | | 96

dvitīya prapāṭhakaḥ  |  prathamo'rdhaḥ
1 2 1 0101   puru tvā dāśivāṃ voce'riragne tava svidā  |
1 2 1 0101  todasyeva śaraṇa ā mahasya  | | 97
1 2 1 0102   pra hotre pūrvyaṃ vaco'gnaye bharatā bṛhat |
1 2 1 0102  vipāṃ jyotīṃṣi bibhrate na vedhase  | | 98
1 2 1 0103   agne vājasya gomata īśānaḥ sahaso yaho  |
1 2 1 0103  asme dhehi jātavedo mahi śravaḥ  | | 99
1 2 1 0104   agne yajiṣṭho adhvare devāṃ devayate yaja  |
1 2 1 0104  hotā mandro vi rājasyati sridhaḥ  | | 100
1 2 1 0105   jajñānaḥ sapta mātṛbhirmedhāmāśāsata śriye  |
1 2 1 0105  ayaṃ dhruvo rayīṇāṃ ciketadā  | | 101
1 2 1 0106   uta syā no divā matiraditirūtyāgamat |
1 2 1 0106  sā śantātā mayaskaradapa sridhaḥ  | | 102
1 2 1 0107   īḍiṣvā hi pratīvyā3ṃ yajasva jātavedasam  |
1 2 1 0107  cariṣṇudhūmamagṛbhītaśociṣam  | | 103
1 2 1 0108   na tasya māyayā ca na ripurīśīta martyaḥ  |
1 2 1 0108  yo agnaye dadāśa havyadātaye  | | 104
1 2 1 0109   apa tyaṃ vṛjinaṃ ripuṃ stenamagne durādhyam  |
1 2 1 0109  daviṣṭhamasya satpate kṛdhī sugam  | | 105
1 2 1 0110   śruṣṭyagne navasya me stomasya vīra viśpate  |
1 2 1 0110  ni māyinastapasā rakṣaso daha  | | 106

1 2 1 0201   pra maṃhiṣṭhāya gāyata ṛtāvne bṛhate śukraśociṣe  |
1 2 1 0201  upastutāso agnaye  | | 107
1 2 1 0202   pra so agne tavotibhiḥ suvīrābhistarati vājakarmabhiḥ  |
1 2 1 0202  yasya tvaṃ sakhyamāvitha  | | 108
1 2 1 0203   taṃ gūrdhayā svarṇaraṃ devāso devamaratiṃ dadhanvire  |
1 2 1 0203  devatrā havyamūhiṣe  | | 109
1 2 1 0204   mā no hṛṇīthā atithiṃ vasuragniḥ purupraśasta eśaḥ  |
1 2 1 0204  yaḥ suhotā svadhvaraḥ  | | 110
1 2 1 0205   bhadro no agnirāhuto bhadrā rātiḥ subhaga bhadro adhvaraḥ  |
1 2 1 0205  bhadrā uta praśastayaḥ  | | 111
1 2 1 0206   yajiṣṭhaṃ tvā vavṛmahe devaṃ devatrā hotāramamartyam  |
1 2 1 0206  asya yajñasya sukratum  | | 112
1 2 1 0207   tadagne dyumnamā bhara yatsāsāhā sadane kaṃ cidatriṇam  |

pāvamāna kāṇḍam
1 5 2 0901   uccā te jātamandhaso divi sadbhūmyā dade  |

1 5 2 1001   pra somāso madacyutaḥ śravase no maghonaḥ  |

1 6 1 0101   upo ṣu jātamapturaṃ gobhirbhaṅgaṃ pariṣkṛtam  |

1 6 1 0108  vavrivāṃsaṃ mahīrapaḥ  | | 494
1 6 1 0109   ayā vītī pari srava yasta indo madeṣvā  |
1 6 1 0109  avāhannavatīrnava  | | 495

1 6 1 0110  svāno arṣa pavitra ā  | | 496

1 6 1 0204  taratsa mandī dhāvati  | | 500
1 6 1 0205   ā pavasva sahasriṇaṃ rayiṃ soma suvīryam  |

1 6 1 0206  ruce jananta sūryam  | | 502

1 6 1 0301   punānaḥ soma dhārayāpo vasāno arṣasi  |

1 6 1 0402   pra kāvyamuśaneva bruvāṇo devo devānāṃ janimā vivakti  |

2 0 0 0309   viśve devā mama śṛṇvantu yajñamubhe rodasī apāṃ napācca manma  |
2 0 0 0309  mā vo vacāṃsi paricakṣyāṇi vocaṃ sumneṣvidvo antamā madema  | | 610

2 0 0 0311  ahannahimanvapastatarda pra vakṣaṇā abhinatparvatānām  | | 612

3 0 0 0001   vidā maghavanvidā gātumanuśaṃsiṣo diśaḥ  |

uttara ārcikaḥ
prathama prapāṭhakaḥ  |  prathamo'rdhaḥ
asitaḥ kāśyapo devalo vā | gāyatrī | pavamānaḥ somaḥ |
4 1 1 01 01   upāsmai gāyatā naraḥ pavamānāyendave  |
4 1 1 01 01  abhi devāṃ iyakṣate  | | 651
4 1 1 01 02   abhi te madhunā payo'tharvāṇo aśiśrayuḥ  |
4 1 1 01 02  devaṃ devāya devayu  | | 652
4 1 1 01 03   sa naḥ pavasva śaṃ gave śaṃ janāya śamarvate  |
4 1 1 01 03  śaṃ rājannoṣadhībhyaḥ  | | 653

kaśyapo mārīcaḥ | gāyatrī | pavamānaḥ somaḥ |
4 1 1 02 01   davidyutatyā rucā pariṣṭobhantyā kṛpā  |
4 1 1 02 01  somāḥ śukrā gavāśiraḥ  | | 654
4 1 1 02 02   hinvāno hetṛbhirhita ā vājaṃ vājyakramīt |
4 1 1 02 02  sīdanto vanuṣo yathā  | | 655
4 1 1 02 03   ṛdhaksoma svastaye saṃjagmāno divā kave  |
4 1 1 02 03  pavasva sūryo dṛśe  | | 656

śataṃ vaikhānasāḥ, gāyatrī, pavamānaḥ somaḥ |
4 1 1 03 01   pavamānasya te kave vājintsargā asṛkṣate  |
4 1 1 03 01  arvanto na śravasyavaḥ  | | 657
4 1 1 03 02   acchā kośaṃ madhuścutamasṛgraṃ vāre avyaye  |
4 1 1 03 02  avāvaśanta dhītayaḥ  | | 658
4 1 1 03 03   acchā samudramindavo'staṃ gāvo na dhenavaḥ  |
4 1 1 03 03  agmannṛtasya yonimā  | | 659

bharadvājo bārhaspatyaḥ, gāyatrī, agniḥ |
4 1 1 04 01   agna ā yāhi vītaye gṛṇāno havyadātaye  |
4 1 1 04 01  ni hotā satsi barhiṣi  | | 660
4 1 1 04 02   taṃ tvā samidbhiraṅgiro ghṛtena vardhayāmasi  |
4 1 1 04 02  bṛhacchocā yaviṣṭhya  | | 661
4 1 1 04 03   sa naḥ pṛthu śravāyyamacchā deva vivāsasi  |
4 1 1 04 03  bṛhadagne suvīryam  | | 662

viśvāmitro gāthinaḥ jamadagnirvā, gāyatrī, mitrāvaruṇau |
4 1 1 05 01   ā no mitrāvaruṇā ghṛtairgavyūtimukṣatam  |
4 1 1 05 01  madhvā rajāṃsi sukratū  | | 663
4 1 1 05 02   uruśaṃsā namovṛdhā mahnā dakṣasya rājathaḥ  |
4 1 1 05 02  drāghiṣṭhābhiḥ śucivratā  | | 664
4 1 1 05 03   gṛṇānā jamadagninā yonāvṛtasya sīdatam  |
4 1 1 05 03  pātaṃ somamṛtāvṛdhā  | | 665

irīmbiṭhiḥ kāṇvaḥ | gāyatrī | indraḥ |
4 1 1 06 03   ā yāhi suṣumā hi ta indra somaṃ pibā imam  |
4 1 1 06 03  edaṃ barhiḥ sado mama  | | 666
4 1 1 06 03   ā tvā brahmayujā harī vahatāmindra keśinā  |
4 1 1 06 03  upa brahmāṇi naḥ śṛṇu  | | 667
4 1 1 06 03   brahmāṇastvā yujā vayaṃ somapāmindra sominaḥ  |
4 1 1 06 03  sutāvanto havāmahe  | | 668

viśvāmitro gāthinaḥ | gāyatrī | indrāgnī |
4 1 1 07 01   indrāgnī ā gataṃ sutaṃ gīrbhirnabho vareṇyam  |
4 1 1 07 01  asya pātaṃ dhiyeṣitā  | | 669
4 1 1 07 02   indrāgnī jarituḥ sacā yajño jigāti cetanaḥ  |
4 1 1 07 02  ayā pātamimaṃ sutam  | | 670
4 1 1 07 03   indramagniṃ kavicchadā yajñasya jūtyā vṛṇe  |
4 1 1 07 03  tā somasyeha tṛmpatām  | | 671

amahīyurāṅgirasaḥ | gāyatrī | pavamānaḥ somaḥ |
4 1 1 08 01   uccā te jātamandhaso divi sadbhūmyā dade  |
4 1 1 08 01  ugraṃ śarma mahi śravaḥ  | | 672
4 1 1 08 02   sa na indrāya yajyave varuṇāya marudbhyaḥ  |
4 1 1 08 02  varivovitpari srava  | | 673
4 1 1 08 03   enā viśvānyarya ā dyumnāni mānuṣāṇām  |
4 1 1 08 03  siṣāsanto vanāmahe  | | 674

4 1 1 09 01   punānaḥ soma dhārayāpo vasāno arṣasi  |
4 1 1 09 01  ā ratnadhā yonimṛtasya sīdasyutso devo hiraṇyayaḥ  | | 675
4 1 1 09 02   duhāna ūdhardivyaṃ madhu priyaṃ pratnaṃ sadhasthamāsadat |
4 1 1 09 02  āpṛcchyaṃ dharuṇaṃ vājyarṣasi nṛbhirdhauto vicakṣaṇaḥ  | | 676

4 1 1 10 01   pra tu drava pari kośaṃ ni ṣīda nṛbhiḥ punāno abhi vājamarṣa  |
4 1 1 10 01  aśvaṃ na tvā vājinaṃ marjayanto'cchā barhī raśanābhirnayanti  | | 677
4 1 1 10 02   svāyudhaḥ pavate deva induraśastihā vṛjanā rakṣamāṇaḥ  |
4 1 1 10 02  pitā devānāṃ janitā sudakṣo viṣṭambho divo dharuṇaḥ pṛthivyāḥ  | | 678
4 1 1 10 03   ṛṣirvipraḥ puraetā janānāmṛbhurdhīra uśanā kāvyena  |
4 1 1 10 03  sa cidviveda nihitaṃ yadāsāmapīcyā3ṃ guhyaṃ nāma gonām  | | 679

4 1 1 11 01   abhi tvā śūra nonumo'dugdhā iva dhenavaḥ  |
4 1 1 11 01  īśānamasya jagataḥ svardṛśamīśānamindra tasthuṣaḥ  | | 680
4 1 1 11 02   na tvāvāṃ anyo divyo na pārthivo na jāto na janiṣyate  |
4 1 1 11 02  aśvāyanto maghavannindra vājino gavyantastvā havāmahe  | | 681

4 1 1 12 01   kayā naścitra ā bhuvadūtī sadāvṛdhaḥ sakhā  |
4 1 1 12 01  kayā śaciṣṭhayā vṛtā  | | 682
4 1 1 12 02   kastvā satyo madānāṃ maṃhiṣṭho matsadandhasaḥ  |
4 1 1 12 02  dṛḍhā cidāruje vasu  | | 683
4 1 1 12 03   abhī ṣu ṇaḥ sakhīnāmavitā jaritṛīṇām  |

4 9 3 0702  yo asmāṃ abhidāsatyadharaṃ gamayā tamaḥ  | | 1868
4 9 3 0703   indrasya bāhū sthavirau yuvānāvanādhṛṣyau supratīkāvasahyau  |
4 9 3 0703  tau yuñjīta prathamau yoga āgate yābhyāṃ jitamasurāṇāṃ saho mahat | | 1869

4 9 3 0801   marmāṇi te varmaṇā cchādayāmi somastvā rājāmṛtenānu vastām  |
4 9 3 0801  urorvarīyo varuṇaste kṛṇotu jayantaṃ tvānu devā madantu  | | 1870
4 9 3 0802   andhā amitrā bhavatāśīrṣāṇo'haya iva  |
4 9 3 0802  teṣāṃ vo agninunnānāmindro hantu varaṃvaram  | | 1871
4 9 3 0803   yo naḥ svo'raṇo yaśca niṣṭyo jighāṃsati  |
4 9 3 0803  devāstaṃ sarve dhūrvantu brahma varma mamāntaraṃ śarma varma mamāntaram  | | 1872

4 9 3 0901   mṛgo na bhīmaḥ kucaro giriṣṭhāḥ parāvata ā jaganthā parasyāḥ  |
4 9 3 0901  sṛkaṃ saṃśāya pavimindra tigmaṃ vi śatrūṃ tāḍhi vi mṛdho nudasva  | | 1873
4 9 3 0902   bhadraṃ karṇebhiḥ śṛṇuyāma devā bhadraṃ paśyemākṣabhiryajatrāḥ  |

4 9 3 0903   svasti na indro vṛddhaśravāḥ svasti naḥ pūṣā viśvavedāḥ  |
4 9 3 0903  svasti nastārkṣyo ariṣṭanemiḥ svasti no bṛhaspatirdadhātu  | |
